#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                                           size_t index, size_t xp, size_t xn,
+                                           size_t yp, size_t yn, double center,
+                                           double previous, double next) {
+    const double cxx = field[index + xp] + field[index - xn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double cyy = field[index + yp] + field[index - yn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double czz = next + previous - 2.0 * center;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
+    }
+}
+
+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t volume) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t x = index % nx;
+        const size_t y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const size_t z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+    }
+}
+
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xy = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const size_t x = xy % nx;
+    const size_t y = xy / nx;
+    const size_t xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const size_t yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        size_t index = zbase * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }

-            }
-        }
+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
     }
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
     }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
 }

+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                                           size_t index, size_t xp, size_t xn,
+                                           size_t yp, size_t yn, double center,
+                                           double previous, double next) {

+    const double cyy = field[index + yp] + field[index - yn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double czz = next + previous - 2.0 * center;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;

+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
     }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step

+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t volume) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t x = index % nx;
+        const size_t y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const size_t z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
     }
 }
 
-// Initialize concentration field
-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
-    
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
-                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xy = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const size_t x = xy % nx;
+    const size_t y = xy / nx;
+    const size_t xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const size_t yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        size_t index = zbase * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+    }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+

+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xy = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const size_t x = xy % nx;
+    const size_t y = xy / nx;
+    const size_t xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const size_t yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        size_t index = zbase * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+    }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}

+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,

+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;

+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
     }

+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t volume) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t x = index % nx;
+        const size_t y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const size_t z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
     }
 }

-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xy = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const size_t x = xy % nx;
+    const size_t y = xy / nx;
+    const size_t xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const size_t yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        size_t index = zbase * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,

+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+    }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}

+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,

+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
     }
 }

+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t volume) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t x = index % nx;
+        const size_t y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const size_t z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
     }
 }

-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xy = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const size_t x = xy % nx;
+    const size_t y = xy / nx;
+    const size_t xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const size_t yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        size_t index = zbase * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+    }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}

The CUDA version builds and passes the initial comparisons, including one-cell dimensions, uneven grids, and odd iteration counts. The default 64³ case runs in about 1 ms versus 34 ms for the original CPU build. Differences are near floating-point roundoff; I’m checking whole-field hashes with contraction disabled and tuning larger grids next.

/bin/bash -lc "g++ -std=c++20 -O3 -march=native -ffp-contract=off -I common -x c++ - -o /tmp/cahn_hilliard_reference <<'CPP'
#include <vector>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include \"results_output.hpp\"
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx=64, ny=0, nz=0;
    int iterations=20;
    for (int i=1; i<argc; ++i) {
        if ("'!strcmp(argv[i],"-x")) nx=atoi(argv[++i]);
        else if (!strcmp(argv[i],"-y")) ny=atoi(argv[++i]);
        else if (!strcmp(argv[i],"-z")) nz=atoi(argv[++i]);
        else if (!strcmp(argv[i],"-i")) iterations=atoi(argv[++i]);
    }
    if (!ny) ny=nx;
    if (!nz) nz=nx;
    size_t n=nx*ny*nz, plane=nx*ny;
    std::vector<double> c(n), b(n), mu(n);
    for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) c[i]=-1.+2.*((((i+1)*1299709)%n)/static_cast<double>(n));
    auto lap = [&](const std::vector<double>& a,size_t x,size_t y,size_t z) {
        size_t i=z*plane+y*nx+x;
        double xx=a[i+(x<nx-1?1:0)]+a[i-(x>0?1:0)]-2.*a[i];
        double yy=a[i+(y<ny-1?nx:0)]+a[i-(y>0?nx:0)]-2.*a[i];
        double zz=a[i+(z<nz-1?plane:0)]+a[i-(z>0?plane:0)]-2.*a[i];
        return xx+yy+zz;
    };
    constexpr double eAA=-(2./9.), eBB=-(2./9.), eAB=2./9.;
    for(int t=0;t<iterations;++t) {
        for(size_t z=0;z<nz;++z) for(size_t y=0;y<ny;++y) for(size_t x=0;x<nx;++x) {
            size_t i=z*plane+y*nx+x; double v=c[i];
            mu[i]=4.5*((v+1.)*eAA+(v-1.)*eBB-2.*v*eAB)+3.*v+v*v*v-0.5*lap(c,x,y,z);
        }
        for(size_t z=0;z<nz;++z) for(size_t y=0;y<ny;++y) for(size_t x=0;x<nx;++x) {
            size_t i=z*plane+y*nx+x; b[i]=c[i]+0.01*lap(mu,x,y,z);
        }
        c.swap(b);
    }
    print_results(c,"Concentration");
}

+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,

+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
     }
 }

+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t volume) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t x = index % nx;
+        const size_t y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const size_t z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
     }
 }

-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xy = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const size_t x = xy % nx;
+    const size_t y = xy / nx;
+    const size_t xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const size_t yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        size_t index = zbase * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+    }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}

-    
+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+template <typename Index>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                                           Index index, Index xp, Index xn,
+                                           Index yp, Index yn, double center,
+                                           double previous, double next) {
+    const double cxx = field[index + xp] + field[index - xn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double cyy = field[index + yp] + field[index - yn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double czz = next + previous - 2.0 * center;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
+    }
+}
+
+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical, typename Index>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             Index nx, Index ny, Index nz, Index volume) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < volume; linear += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Index index = static_cast<Index>(linear);
+        const Index x = index % nx;
+        const Index y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const Index z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+    }
+}
+
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip, typename Index>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              Index nx, Index ny, Index nz) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const Index x = xy % nx;
+    const Index y = xy / nx;
+    const Index xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const Index yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        Index index = static_cast<Index>(zbase) * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }

+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename Index>
+void launchStepImpl(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, Index nx, Index ny,
+                Index nz, Index volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
     }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {

+    // Keep a 64-bit path for grids that exceed that range.
+    if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
+        launchStepImpl(cold, cnew, mu, static_cast<unsigned>(nx),
+                       static_cast<unsigned>(ny), static_cast<unsigned>(nz),
+                       static_cast<unsigned>(volume), useColumns, grid, stream);
+    } else {
+        launchStepImpl(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume, useColumns, grid, stream);
     }
 }

-    
+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+template <typename Index>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                                           Index index, Index xp, Index xn,
+                                           Index yp, Index yn, double center,
+                                           double previous, double next) {
+    const double cxx = field[index + xp] + field[index - xn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double cyy = field[index + yp] + field[index - yn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double czz = next + previous - 2.0 * center;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                const double cv = c[idx];

-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
+    }
+}
+
+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical, typename Index>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             Index nx, Index ny, Index nz, Index volume) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < volume; linear += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Index index = static_cast<Index>(linear);
+        const Index x = index % nx;
+        const Index y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const Index z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+    }
+}
+
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip, typename Index>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              Index nx, Index ny, Index nz) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const Index x = xy % nx;
+    const Index y = xy / nx;
+    const Index xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const Index yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        Index index = static_cast<Index>(zbase) * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }

+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename Index>
+void launchStepImpl(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, Index nx, Index ny,
+                Index nz, Index volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
     }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    // Most fields fit in 32-bit indices, avoiding expensive 64-bit division.
+    // Keep a 64-bit path for grids that exceed that range.
+    if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
+        launchStepImpl(cold, cnew, mu, static_cast<unsigned>(nx),
+                       static_cast<unsigned>(ny), static_cast<unsigned>(nz),
+                       static_cast<unsigned>(volume), useColumns, grid, stream);
+    } else {
+        launchStepImpl(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume, useColumns, grid, stream);
     }
 }

-// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
-    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
-    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
-    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
-    
-    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
-    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
-    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
-    
+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+template <typename Index>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                                           Index index, Index xp, Index xn,
+                                           Index yp, Index yn, double center,
+                                           double previous, double next) {
+    const double cxx = field[index + xp] + field[index - xn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double cyy = field[index + yp] + field[index - yn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double czz = next + previous - 2.0 * center;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {
+    if constexpr (Chemical) {
+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
+    }
+}
+
+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical, typename Index>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             Index nx, Index ny, Index nz, Index volume) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < volume; linear += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Index index = static_cast<Index>(linear);
+        const Index x = index % nx;
+        const Index y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const Index z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian<Index>(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+    }
+}
+
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip, typename Index>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              Index nx, Index ny, Index nz) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const Index x = xy % nx;
+    const Index y = xy / nx;
+    const Index xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const Index yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        Index index = static_cast<Index>(zbase) * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
-        }
+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename Index>
+void launchStepImpl(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, Index nx, Index ny,
+                Index nz, Index volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
     }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
 }

-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
-            }
-        }
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                size_t nz, size_t volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    // Most fields fit in 32-bit indices, avoiding expensive 64-bit division.
+    // Keep a 64-bit path for grids that exceed that range.
+    if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
+        launchStepImpl(cold, cnew, mu, static_cast<unsigned>(nx),
+                       static_cast<unsigned>(ny), static_cast<unsigned>(nz),
+                       static_cast<unsigned>(volume), useColumns, grid, stream);
+    } else {
+        launchStepImpl(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume, useColumns, grid, stream);
     }
 }

+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+        return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                      - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+               + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - 0.5 * lap;
+    } else {
+        return cold[index] + 0.01 * lap;
+    }
+}
+
+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical, typename Index>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             Index nx, Index ny, Index nz, Index volume) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < volume; linear += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Index index = static_cast<Index>(linear);
+        const Index x = index % nx;
+        const Index y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const Index z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian<Index>(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+    }
+}
+
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip, typename Index>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              Index nx, Index ny, Index nz) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const Index x = xy % nx;
+    const Index y = xy / nx;
+    const Index xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const Index yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        Index index = static_cast<Index>(zbase) * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }

+         index < volume; index += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((index + 1) * 1299709) % volume)
+                              / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[index] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename Index>
+void launchStepImpl(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, Index nx, Index ny,
+                Index nz, Index volume, bool useColumns, dim3 grid,
+                cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (useColumns) {
+        columnStencil<true, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+        columnStencil<false, 4><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        pointStencil<true><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(cold, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, volume);
+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
     }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
 }

 }

template <unsigned Strip>
void measure(double* a,double* b,double* mu,unsigned nx,unsigned ny,unsigned nz,unsigned block,cudaStream_t s) {
    unsigned plane=nx*ny, volume=plane*nz;
    dim3 grid(Strip ? (plane+block-1)/block : (volume+block-1)/block, Strip ? (nz+Strip-1)/Strip : 1);
    cudaGraph_t graph; cudaGraphExec_t executable;
    checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(s,cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),\"capture\");
    for(int t=0;t<40;++t) {
        if constexpr (Strip==0) {
            pointStencil<true><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(a,a,mu,nx,ny,nz,volume);
            pointStencil<false><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(mu,a,b,nx,ny,nz,volume);
        } else {
            columnStencil<true,Strip><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(a,a,mu,nx,ny,nz);
            columnStencil<false,Strip><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(mu,a,b,nx,ny,nz);
        }
        std::swap(a,b);
    }
    checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(s,&graph),\"capture end\");
    checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable,graph,nullptr,nullptr,0),\"instantiate\");
    checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable,s),\"warm up\");
    cudaEvent_t start,end; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&end);
    cudaEventRecord(start,s);
    for(int i=0;i<3;++i) checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable,s),\"launch\");
    cudaEventRecord(end,s); checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(end),\"sync\");
    float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,start,end);
    printf(\"strip %2u block %3u: %9.1f MUpdates/s\\n\",Strip,block,volume*120./(ms*1000.));
    fflush(stdout);
    cudaEventDestroy(start);cudaEventDestroy(end);cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
}
int main() {
    cudaStream_t s; checkCuda(cudaStreamCreate(&s),\"stream\");
    for(unsigned n : {32u,64u,128u,256u,512u}) {
        unsigned volume=n*n*n;
        double* storage;checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage,size_t(volume)*24),\"allocate\");
        initializeConcentration<<<1024,128,0,s>>>(storage,volume);
        printf(\"GRID %u"'^3'"\\n\",n);
        for(unsigned block : {64u,128u,256u}) {
            measure<0>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
            measure<1>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
            measure<2>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
            measure<4>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
            measure<8>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
            measure<16>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
        }
        cudaFree(storage);
    }
    cudaStreamDestroy(s);
}

+template <typename Index>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                                           Index index, Index xp, Index xn,

+template <bool Chemical>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double stencilResult(double center, double lap,
+                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                                                size_t index) {

+        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);

+// For small or thin grids, one thread per cell exposes enough parallel work.
+template <bool Chemical, typename Index>
+__global__ void pointStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                             const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                             double* __restrict__ output,
+                             Index nx, Index ny, Index nz, Index volume) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t linear = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         linear < volume; linear += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Index index = static_cast<Index>(linear);
+        const Index x = index % nx;
+        const Index y = (index / nx) % ny;
+        const Index z = index / plane;
+        const double center = field[index];
+        const double previous = field[index - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+        const double lap = laplacian<Index>(field, index, x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0,
+                                     x > 0 ? 1 : 0, y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0,
+                                     y > 0 ? nx : 0, center, previous, next);
+        output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+    }
+}
+
+// Adjacent threads traverse adjacent XY cells. Marching through a short Z strip
+// reuses the two Z neighbors in registers instead of repeatedly loading them.
+template <bool Chemical, unsigned Strip, typename Index>
+__global__ void columnStencil(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                              const double* __restrict__ cold,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              Index nx, Index ny, Index nz) {
+    const Index plane = nx * ny;
+    const Index xy = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (xy >= plane) return;
+    const Index x = xy % nx;
+    const Index y = xy / nx;
+    const Index xp = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index xn = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const Index yp = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+    const Index yn = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+    for (size_t zbase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Strip; zbase < nz;
+         zbase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * Strip) {
+        Index index = static_cast<Index>(zbase) * plane + xy;
+        double center = field[index];
+        double previous = field[index - (zbase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < Strip; ++k) {
+            const size_t z = zbase + k;
+            if (z < nz) {
+                const double next = field[index + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double lap = laplacian(field, index, xp, xn, yp, yn,
+                                             center, previous, next);
+                output[index] = stencilResult<Chemical>(center, lap, cold, index);
+                previous = center;
+                center = next;
+                index += plane;
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void launchStepImpl(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu, Index nx, Index ny,

+        pointStencil<false><<<grid, 128, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz, volume);
     }
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
 }

#define main benchmarkMain

template <unsigned Strip>
void measure(double* a,double* b,double* mu,unsigned nx,unsigned ny,unsigned nz,unsigned block,cudaStream_t s) {
    unsigned plane=nx*ny, volume=plane*nz;
    dim3 grid(Strip ? (plane+block-1)/block : (volume+block-1)/block, Strip ? (nz+Strip-1)/Strip : 1);
    cudaGraph_t graph; cudaGraphExec_t executable;
    checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(s,cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),\"capture\");
    for(int t=0;t<40;++t) {
        if constexpr (Strip==0) {
            pointStencil<true><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(a,a,mu,nx,ny,nz,volume);
            pointStencil<false><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(mu,a,b,nx,ny,nz,volume);
        } else {
            columnStencil<true,Strip><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(a,a,mu,nx,ny,nz);
            columnStencil<false,Strip><<<grid,block,0,s>>>(mu,a,b,nx,ny,nz);
        }
        std::swap(a,b);
    }
    checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(s,&graph),\"capture end\");
    checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable,graph,nullptr,nullptr,0),\"instantiate\");
    checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable,s),\"warm up\");
    cudaEvent_t start,end; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&end);
    cudaEventRecord(start,s);
    for(int i=0;i<3;++i) checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executable,s),\"launch\");
    cudaEventRecord(end,s); checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(end),\"sync\");
    float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms,start,end);
    printf(\"strip %2u block %3u: %9.1f MUpdates/s\\n\",Strip,block,volume*120./(ms*1000.));
    fflush(stdout);
    cudaEventDestroy(start);cudaEventDestroy(end);cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
}
int main() {
    cudaStream_t s; checkCuda(cudaStreamCreate(&s),\"stream\");
    for(unsigned n : {32u,64u,128u,256u,512u}) {
        unsigned volume=n*n*n;
        double* storage;checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage,size_t(volume)*24),\"allocate\");
        initializeConcentration<<<1024,128,0,s>>>(storage,volume);
        printf(\"GRID %u"'^3'"\\n\",n);
        for(unsigned block : {64u,128u,256u}) {
            measure<0>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
            measure<1>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);

            measure<16>(storage,storage+volume,storage+2*volume,n,n,n,block,s);
        }
        cudaFree(storage);
    }
    cudaStreamDestroy(s);
}

+// The benchmark uses unit grid spacing. Keep each directional difference and
+// their addition in the same order as the original double-precision stencil.
+template <typename Index>
+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ field,
+                                           Index index, Index xp, Index xn,
+                                           Index yp, Index yn, double center,
+                                           double previous, double next) {
+    const double cxx = field[index + xp] + field[index - xn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double cyy = field[index + yp] + field[index - yn] - 2.0 * center;
+    const double czz = next + previous - 2.0 * center;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
